## 2. The pattern and its gate
- Preconditions: a "Preconditions" heading and plain bullets only, in setup order (Sign in → records and their state → browser → design pictures → starting screen), the Blocked line last: "If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".". No Scenario line, no {placeholder}, no "For example", no setup-file link, no who-to-ask or generic line, no ZZAUTOTEST example name.
- Records keep one plain name used the same way in preconditions, steps and results: term A, customer B, vendor A, work order 1, invoice A, user B, role A; one location is "the test location", two are location A and location B; a number written down before the test is number A; a date worked out from today is date A; the signed-in user's own name is "your user name".
- A value the result depends on became a plain fact (it was the example): D-25: {Amount-A} = $500.00; D-27: {Amount-A} = $5,000.00; D-40: {Amount-A} = $5,000.00; D-36: {Amount-A} = $500.00; S5-22: {Text-A} = "Prepaid"; S7-05: {Text-A} = "Prepaid"; S10-03: {Text-A} = "Prepaid"; S3-12: {Text-A} = a 32-character name; S1-22: {Address-A} = the Credit Terms page's web address; S12-10: {Hours-A} = the difference in hours.
- Steps: the same actions, with the plain names ("Type the name of term A in Name.").
- Expected Result: "Step N:" then one bullet per sentence of the old result (a "; " outside quotes and brackets also splits), then one blank paragraph, then the note (if any), the exact quotes with their From lines, References from Design and the AUTOMATION line, unchanged.
- `authoring/build.py` gate (replaces the Scenario gate; every other gate kept): the shape above, checked on the rendered HTML; the tail from "Exact quotes from the source" to the AUTOMATION line equal to `before/proposals.json`; every User Feedback Summary message still word for word in the pattern steps and results.

### Hand-written forms (the rules could not phrase these plainly; meaning kept)
- S1-22 precondition: "You have the Credit Terms page's web address, {Address-A}, copied earlier from an Admin's browser." → "Have the Credit Terms page's web address, copied earlier from an Admin's browser."
- S2-22 precondition: "Before you start, the Active tab's count reads {Count-A} and the Inactive tab's {Count-B} (write them down)." → "Before you start, write down the Active tab's count (number A) and the Inactive tab's count (number B)."
- DATA-24 precondition: "Before you start, the Active tab's count reads {Count-A} and the Inactive tab's {Count-B} (write them down)." → "Before you start, write down the Active tab's count (number A) and the Inactive tab's count (number B)."
- S4-08 precondition: "Before you start, Net 30's Customers count reads {Count-A} (write it down)." → "Before you start, write down Net 30's Customers count (number A)."
- S3-12 precondition: "A 32-character text, {Text-A}." → "Have a name ready that is 32 characters long."
- S12-10 precondition: "You are enrolled at {Location-B}, in a time zone {Hours-A} hours apart from {Location-A}." → "Make sure you are also enrolled at location B, in a time zone a whole number of hours apart from the test location, and note that difference in hours."
- DATA-18 precondition: "Today's date at {Location-B} is {Date-A}; {Date-B} is {Date-A} plus 30 days." → "Note today's date at location B (date A)." / "Work out date B: date A plus 30 days."
- DATA-19 precondition: "Today's date at {Location-A} is {Date-A}; {Date-B} is {Date-A} plus 365 days, and {Date-C} is day 31 of the month after {Date-A}, or that month's last day if it is shorter." → "Note today's date at the test location (date A)." / "Work out date B: date A plus 365 days." / "Work out date C: day 31 of the month after date A, or that month's last day if it is shorter."
- D-13 precondition: "Today on the calendar of the location you work in is {Date-A}, and {Date-B} is the date 14 days later." → "Note today's date on the calendar of the location you work in (date A)." / "Work out date B: the date 14 days later."
- D-14 precondition: "Today on the calendar of the location you work in is {Date-A}, and {Date-B} is the 10th of the following month." → "Note today's date on the calendar of the location you work in (date A)." / "Work out date B: the 10th of the following month."
- DATA-23 precondition: "Net 15 is held by {Count-A} customers and Net 20 by {Count-B}, written down; three of the Net 15 customers are {Customer-A}, {Customer-B} and {Customer-C}." → "Make sure three of the customers on Net 15 are customer A, customer B and customer C." / "Before you start, write down how many customers hold Net 15 (number A) and how many hold Net 20 (number B)."
- S11-02 precondition: "Before the release reached production, these were written down on production (with screenshots): customer {Customer-A}'s term and credit limit, vendor {Vendor-A}'s term and credit limit, and invoice {Invoice-A}'s term and due date, and vendor invoice {Vendor-invoice-A}'s due date." → "Have these written down on production from before the release reached production (with screenshots): customer A's term and credit limit, vendor A's term and credit limit, invoice A's term and due date, and vendor invoice A's due date."
- D-38 precondition: "The log cannot be loaded (the setup file says how to make it fail on the test shop)." → "Make sure the Credit Terms Log cannot be loaded on the test shop."
- S1-36 Step 1 result → "The role editor opens." / "There is no new toggle for credit terms." / "Credit Terms is governed by the existing "Finance" sub-row under "Settings"." / "Move Customers is governed by the existing "Customers" row's "Create & Edit" column and the "View and Manage AP/AR Data" cross toggle." / "The Settings sub-toggles are the same six as before the release (App Settings, Service, Parts, Finance, Data Import, View/Manage Wages)."
- D-33 Step 1 result → "Under "Invoice: INV-" followed by work order 1's number, the lines read "Invoice date: Oct 5, 2026" and "Due date: Nov 10, 2026"." / "The reference chips read "Work Order" with work order 1's number, "Customer PO …" and "Terms" with term A's name, the term name whole." / "The layout is today's (compare ct-p9-02-10th-of-next-month-invoice-oct-5-due-nov-10.png and today's ct-p9-01-an-invoice-today-net-30.png; the orange outline on the picture is not part of the screen)."
- S12-10 Step 4 result → "The newest row shows your user name under User." / "It shows the date and time of the change as clocks at the test location read them."
- S12-10 Step 8 result → "The same row now shows the time later by the difference in hours (as clocks at location B read it)." / "The date changes too if the move crosses midnight."

## 3. Old and new, every case
Old: the Scenario line, the preconditions (with their "For example" lines), steps and results before this pass. New: as they will read in TestRail. The quotes, From lines, References from Design and AUTOMATION line are not repeated (identical, section 1).

### S1-01 · C723865 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723865
- Title (old = new): Credit Terms sits under Payment Methods in Settings and opens on Active
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop still has every standard credit term switched on, and you want to find the Credit Terms page in Settings.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - All twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Starting screen: the Settings page.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure all twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Start on the Settings page.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. In the Settings menu, find the Finance group.
  2. Click "Credit Terms".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the Finance group shows "Credit Terms" directly below "Payment Methods".
  - Step 2: the Credit Terms page opens. Its header reads "Credit Terms", with a primary "New Credit Term" button at the top right and a "⋯" menu beside it. Below the header are two tabs, "Active" and "Inactive", each followed by the number of terms in it, and the Active tab is selected.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The Finance group shows "Credit Terms" directly below "Payment Methods".
  - Step 2:
    - The Credit Terms page opens.
    - Its header reads "Credit Terms", with a primary "New Credit Term" button at the top right and a "⋯" menu beside it.
    - Below the header are two tabs, "Active" and "Inactive", each followed by the number of terms in it, and the Active tab is selected.

### S1-02 · C723866 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723866
- Title (old = new): Both tabs show the same columns at the same widths
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has switched off at least one credit term, so both the Active and the Inactive tab have rows to compare.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - At least one term is deactivated, {Term-A}. _For example: {Term-A} is Net 50._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure at least one term is deactivated, term A.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Look at the column headings of the table on the Active tab.
  2. Click the "Inactive" tab.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the table has one heading row reading Name, Due, Customers and Vendors, followed by a column with no heading that holds a "⋯" button on every row. There is one table only (no separate sections for standard terms and the shop's own terms).
  - Step 2: the Inactive tab shows {Term-A} in a table with the same four headings and the unlabeled "⋯" column, and every column is as wide as on the Active tab (the headings do not move when you switch tabs).
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The table has one heading row reading Name, Due, Customers and Vendors, followed by a column with no heading that holds a "⋯" button on every row.
    - There is one table only (no separate sections for standard terms and the shop's own terms).
  - Step 2:
    - The Inactive tab shows term A in a table with the same four headings and the unlabeled "⋯" column, and every column is as wide as on the Active tab (the headings do not move when you switch tabs).

### S1-03 · C723867 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723867
- Title (old = new): The Active tab lists terms in the fixed order, Credit Hold last
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop uses every standard term plus five terms of its own, and you check the order the Active tab lists them in.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - All twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - The shop has five active terms of its own: {Term-A} due 10 days after the invoice date, {Term-B} due 30 days after the invoice date, {Term-C} due on day 1 of the following month, {Term-D} due on day 22 of the following month, and {Term-E} due on day 1 of the following month. {Term-E}'s name comes after {Term-C}'s in A to Z order. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 Ten, {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 Thirty, {Term-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 First A, {Term-D} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 Twentysecond and {Term-E} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 First B._
  - Other terms of the shop (from other tests) may also be listed; the checks below are about the order of the named rows.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure all twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Make sure the shop has five active terms of its own: term A due 10 days after the invoice date, term B due 30 days after the invoice date, term C due on day 1 of the following month, term D due on day 22 of the following month, and term E due on day 1 of the following month. Term E's name comes after term C's in A to Z order.
  - Other terms may also be listed; check only the order of the named rows.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Without clicking any column heading, read the Name column from top to bottom.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: COD is the first row, Due on Receipt the second and Credit Hold the last. In between, the Net terms (standard and the shop's own) run by number of days, lowest first: Net 7, then {Term-A} (10 days), then Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, then {Term-B} (30 days, after Net 30 because ties sort A to Z by name), then Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120. After every Net term come the day-of-the-following-month terms by day: {Term-C}, then {Term-E} (both day 1, A to Z by name), then {Term-D} (day 22).
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - COD is the first row, Due on Receipt the second and Credit Hold the last.
    - In between, the Net terms (standard and the shop's own) run by number of days, lowest first: Net 7, then term A (10 days), then Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, then term B (30 days, after Net 30 because ties sort A to Z by name), then Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120.
    - After every Net term come the day-of-the-following-month terms by day: term C, then term E (both day 1, A to Z by name), then term D (day 22).

### S1-04 · C723868 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723868
- Title (old = new): The Inactive tab uses the same order as the Active tab
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has switched off two standard Net terms and one term of its own, and you check their order on the Inactive tab.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - Net 60, Net 7 and the shop's own {Term-A}, due on day 5 of the following month, are deactivated. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-04 Fifth._
  - Other terms of the shop (from other tests) may also be listed; the checks below are about the order of the named rows.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure Net 60, Net 7 and the shop's own term A, due on day 5 of the following month, are deactivated.
  - Other terms may also be listed; check only the order of the named rows.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "Inactive" tab.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: reading down the Inactive tab, Net 7 comes before Net 60, which comes before {Term-A}; the tab's count equals the number of rows on it.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - Reading down the Inactive tab, Net 7 comes before Net 60, which comes before term A.
    - The tab's count equals the number of rows on it.

### S1-05 · C723869 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723869
- Title (old = new): Clicking Name sorts A to Z, and again Z to A
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has every standard term switched on, and you sort the list by name.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - All twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure all twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.

- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "Name" column heading.
  2. Click the "Name" column heading again.

  - Step 1: the Name column reads A to Z from top to bottom: COD, Credit Hold and Due on Receipt come before the Net terms, and every name is at or after the one above it in the alphabet.

  - Step 1:
    - The Name column reads A to Z from top to bottom: COD, Credit Hold and Due on Receipt come before the Net terms, and every name is at or after the one above it in the alphabet.
  - Step 2:
    - The order reverses, Z to A: the Net terms (and any later names) come first, and Due on Receipt, Credit Hold and COD are the last three rows in that order.

### S1-06 · C723870 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723870
- Title (old = new): Clicking Customers sorts lowest count first, and again highest first
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has three terms of its own held by one, two and three customers, and you sort the list by customer count.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - The shop's own terms {Term-A}, {Term-B} and {Term-C} are held by exactly 1, 2 and 3 customers (new terms hold only this case's customers). _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-06 One, {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-06 Two and {Term-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-06 Three, each due 14 days after the invoice date._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure the shop's own terms A, B and C are held by exactly 1, 2 and 3 customers (new terms hold only this case's customers).
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "Customers" column heading.
  2. Click the "Customers" column heading again.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the Customers column reads from top to bottom in rising order (every number is the same as or higher than the one above it), so {Term-A} (1) is above {Term-B} (2), which is above {Term-C} (3).
  - Step 2: the order reverses: every number is the same as or lower than the one above it, and {Term-C} (3) is above {Term-B} (2), which is above {Term-A} (1).
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The Customers column reads from top to bottom in rising order (every number is the same as or higher than the one above it), so term A (1) is above term B (2), which is above term C (3).
  - Step 2:
    - The order reverses: every number is the same as or lower than the one above it, and term C (3) is above term B (2), which is above term A (1).

### S1-08 · C723872 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723872
- Title (old = new): Clicking Due sorts in the page's usual order, and again in reverse
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop uses every standard term plus one day-of-the-following-month term of its own, and you sort the list by Due.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - All twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - The shop has its own active term {Term-A}, due on day 10 of the following month. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-08 Tenth._
  - Other terms of the shop (from other tests) may also be listed; the checks below are about the order of the named rows.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure all twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Have the shop's own active term A, due on day 10 of the following month.
  - Other terms may also be listed; check only the order of the named rows.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "Name" column heading.
  2. Click the "Due" column heading.
  3. Click the "Due" column heading again.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the rows are sorted by name A to Z.
  - Step 2: the rows return to the page's usual order: COD first, Due on Receipt second, the Net terms by number of days, then the day-of-the-following-month terms with {Term-A} among them, Credit Hold last.
  - Step 3: the order reverses: Credit Hold first, then the day-of-the-following-month terms (highest day first, {Term-A} among them), then the Net terms from the longest down to Net 7, then Due on Receipt, then COD last.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The rows are sorted by name A to Z.
  - Step 2:
    - The rows return to the page's usual order: COD first, Due on Receipt second, the Net terms by number of days, then the day-of-the-following-month terms with term A among them, Credit Hold last.
  - Step 3:
    - The order reverses: Credit Hold first, then the day-of-the-following-month terms (highest day first, term A among them), then the Net terms from the longest down to Net 7, then Due on Receipt, then COD last.

### S1-10 · C723874 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723874

    - Net 7 reads "7 days after the invoice date", Net 15 "15 days after the invoice date", Net 20 "20 days after the invoice date", Net 30 "30 days after the invoice date", Net 45 "45 days after the invoice date", Net 50 "50 days after the invoice date", Net 60 "60 days after the invoice date", Net 90 "90 days after the invoice date", Net 120 "120 days after the invoice date"
    - Credit Hold reads "Invoicing is blocked".

### S1-11 · C723875 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723875
- Title (old = new): The Due column reads 1 day and ordinal days correctly for the shop's terms
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has made terms of its own due 1 day, 365 days, and on many different days of the following month.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - The shop has its own active terms: {Term-A} due 1 day after the invoice date, {Term-B} due 365 days after, and day-of-the-following-month terms {Term-C} (day 1), {Term-D} (day 2), {Term-E} (day 3), {Term-F} (day 11), {Term-G} (day 12), {Term-H} (day 13), {Term-J} (day 21), {Term-K} (day 22), {Term-L} (day 23) and {Term-M} (day 31). _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-11 N1, {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-11 N365 and {Term-C} to {Term-M} are ZZAUTOTEST S1-11 D1, D2, D3, D11, D12, D13, D21, D22, D23 and D31._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure the shop has its own active terms: term A due 1 day after the invoice date, term B due 365 days after, and day-of-the-following-month terms C (day 1), D (day 2), E (day 3), F (day 11), G (day 12), H (day 13), J (day 21), K (day 22), L (day 23) and M (day 31).
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Read the Due column for {Term-A} to {Term-M}.  →  Read the Due column for term A to term M.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: {Term-A} reads "1 day after the invoice date" (singular); {Term-B} reads "365 days after the invoice date"; {Term-C} "1st of the following month", {Term-D} "2nd of the following month", {Term-E} "3rd of the following month", {Term-F} "11th of the following month", {Term-G} "12th of the following month", {Term-H} "13th of the following month", {Term-J} "21st of the following month", {Term-K} "22nd of the following month", {Term-L} "23rd of the following month", {Term-M} "31st of the following month".
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - Term A reads "1 day after the invoice date" (singular).
    - Term B reads "365 days after the invoice date"
    - Term C "1st of the following month", term D "2nd of the following month", term E "3rd of the following month", term F "11th of the following month", term G "12th of the following month", term H "13th of the following month", term J "21st of the following month", term K "22nd of the following month", term L "23rd of the following month", term M "31st of the following month".

  - The shop has its own active term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-16 Fourteen._
  - The shop has its own active term {Term-B}, due on day 10 of the following month. _For example: {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-16 Tenth._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  - Have the shop's own active term A, due 14 days after the invoice date.
  - Have the shop's own active term B, due on day 10 of the following month.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

    - Net 7, Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120, term A and term B show their name only: no info icon and no lock icon (COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold are the only rows with them).

### S1-19 · C723883 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723883
- Title (old = new): Customers and Vendors counts are right aligned and show 0 when none
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has just made a new term that no customer or vendor uses yet, while Net 30 is in use.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - The shop's own term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, is held by no customer and no vendor (it is new). _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-19 Empty._
  - At least one customer holds Net 30.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure the shop's own term A, due 14 days after the invoice date, is held by no customer and no vendor (it is new).
  - Make sure at least one customer holds Net 30.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Look at the Customers and Vendors cells of the {Term-A} row.  →  Look at the Customers and Vendors cells of the term A row.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: both cells read "0" (not blank, not a dash), right aligned in their columns. The count is right aligned, its last digit lined up with the 0 of the {Term-A} row.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - Both cells read "0" (not blank, not a dash), right aligned in their columns.
    - The count is right aligned, its last digit lined up with the 0 of the term A row.

### S1-20 · C723884 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723884
- Title (old = new): A long term name wraps onto a second line and is never cut off
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop's default term has the longest name allowed, and you view the list in a narrow browser window.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - The shop's own term {Term-A} has a 31-character name, due 14 days after the invoice date, and is the default for new customers. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-20 Long Name 0123 (31 characters)._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure the shop's own term A has a 31-character name, due 14 days after the invoice date, and is the default for new customers.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Look at the {Term-A} row's Name cell, with the browser window at its narrowest desktop width (for example 1280 pixels).  →  Look at the term A row's Name cell, with the browser window at its narrowest desktop width (for example 1280 pixels).
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the whole name shows, every character, wrapping onto a second line if it does not fit; no "..." and no cut-off letters. The "Default" tag comes after the end of the name.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The whole name shows, every character, wrapping onto a second line if it does not fit.
    - No "..." and no cut-off letters.
    - The "Default" tag comes after the end of the name.

### S1-21 · C723885 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723885
- Title (old = new): Switching location reloads the page with the same terms and counts
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: You work at two locations of the same shop, whose default term of its own is held by two customers.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - You are enrolled at two locations, {Location-A} and {Location-B}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 and {Location-B} is Staging Lethbridge - 4310._
  - The shop's own term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, is the default and is held by 2 customers. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-21 Fourteen._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure you are also enrolled at a second location, location B.
  - Make sure the shop's own term A, due 14 days after the invoice date, is the default and is held by 2 customers.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Write down every row's name, Customers count and Vendors count, and which row has the Default tag.
  2. Switch location to {Location-B} with the location switcher at the top right.  →  Switch location to location B with the location switcher at the top right.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: {Term-A} shows the Default tag and 2 customers.
  - Step 2: the page reloads and shows exactly the same rows in the same order, with the same Customers and Vendors counts and the Default tag still on {Term-A}. The terms and the default belong to the whole organization, not to a location.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - Term A shows the Default tag and 2 customers.
  - Step 2:
    - The page reloads and shows exactly the same rows in the same order, with the same Customers and Vendors counts and the Default tag still on term A.
    - The terms and the default belong to the whole organization, not to a location.

### S1-22 · C723886 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723886
- Title (old = new): Without Settings Finance a user cannot see or open Credit Terms
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: A user whose role has every Settings page except Finance tries to reach the Credit Terms page.
  - You are signed in as {User-A}, whose role has every Settings page except Finance. _For example: {User-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-22 User, on a custom role ZZAUTOTEST S1-22 No Finance._
  - You have the Credit Terms page's web address, {Address-A}, copied earlier from an Admin's browser. _For example: {Address-A} is the address shown in an Admin's browser while the Credit Terms page is open._
  - Starting screen: the Settings page.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as a user whose role has every Settings page except Finance.
  - Have the Credit Terms page's web address, copied earlier from an Admin's browser.
  - Start on the Settings page.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Look at the Settings menu.
  2. Paste {Address-A} into the browser's address bar.  →  Paste the Credit Terms page's web address into the browser's address bar.
  3. Press Enter.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: there is no Finance group (or it has no items), so neither "Payment Methods" nor "Credit Terms" shows.
  - Step 2: the address is in the address bar.
  - Step 3: the Credit Terms page does not show; the app behaves as it does when this user opens the Payment Methods address (the same redirect or not-found page). No "New Credit Term" button is reachable.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - There is no Finance group (or it has no items), so neither "Payment Methods" nor "Credit Terms" shows.
  - Step 2:
    - The address is in the address bar.
  - Step 3:
    - The Credit Terms page does not show.
    - The app behaves as it does when this user opens the Payment Methods address (the same redirect or not-found page).
    - No "New Credit Term" button is reachable.

### S1-23 · C723887 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723887

- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure all twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Make sure no term is deactivated (every standard term is active and the shop has no inactive term of its own).
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.

- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the tab label shows a count of 0, and in place of the table the line "No inactive terms" shows.

  - Step 1:
    - The tab label shows a count of 0, and in place of the table the line "No inactive terms" shows.

### S1-25 · C723889 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723889
- Title (old = new): With every term deactivated, Active keeps the three fixed terms and the default
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop uses only two credit terms of its own and has switched off every other term it can.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - The shop created two terms of its own: {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, and {Term-B}, due on the 10th of the following month. It has no other terms of its own. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-25 Fourteen and {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-25 Tenth._
  - {Term-A} is the default for new customers.
  - Every term that can be switched off is deactivated: Net 7, Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120 and {Term-B}.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure the shop created two terms of its own: term A, due 14 days after the invoice date, and term B, due on the 10th of the following month. It has no other terms of its own.
  - Make sure term A is the default for new customers.
  - Make sure every term that can be switched off is deactivated: Net 7, Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120 and term B.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Read the rows on the Active tab.
  2. Click the "Inactive" tab.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: Active lists exactly COD, Due on Receipt, {Term-A} (with the Default tag) and Credit Hold, in that order, and its count reads 4.
  - Step 2: Inactive lists Net 7, Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120 and {Term-B}, in that order, and its count reads 10.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - Active lists exactly COD, Due on Receipt, term A (with the Default tag) and Credit Hold, in that order, and its count reads 4.
  - Step 2:
    - Inactive lists Net 7, Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120 and term B, in that order, and its count reads 10.

### S1-26 · C723890 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723890
- Title (old = new): A term held only by an invoice shows 0 and 0 but is still in use
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop's own term was used on an invoice, and that customer has since been moved to another term.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - The shop's own term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, is held by no customer and no vendor today, but one invoice, {Invoice-A}, was created with it (its customer was later moved to another term). _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-26 Fourteen and {Invoice-A} is the invoice of work order S2-15440._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure the shop's own term A, due 14 days after the invoice date, is held by no customer and no vendor today, but one invoice was created with it (its customer was later moved to another term), so the term is still in use.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Look at the {Term-A} row's Customers and Vendors cells.  →  Look at the term A row's Customers and Vendors cells.
  2. Click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  Click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  3. Hover "Edit".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: both read 0.
  - Step 2: the menu shows "Edit" and "Delete Credit Term" greyed out.
  - Step 3: the hover reads "Used by 1 invoice" with "Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." below it.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - Both read 0.
  - Step 2:
    - The menu shows "Edit" and "Delete Credit Term" greyed out.
  - Step 3:
    - The hover reads "Used by 1 invoice" with "Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." below it.

### S1-27 · C723891 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723891
- Title (old = new): While the list loads, the Inactive tab never says "No inactive terms"
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has switched off at least one term, and you open the Inactive tab before the list has loaded.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - At least one term is deactivated.
  - Starting screen: the Settings page.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**

  - Make sure at least one term is deactivated.
  - Start on the Settings page.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click "Credit Terms".
  2. Click the "Inactive" tab as soon as it can be clicked, before the rows appear.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the page starts loading.
  - Step 2: while the rows are still loading, the line "No inactive terms" does not show at any moment; once loaded, the inactive terms appear.
- **New Expected Result**

  - Step 2:

    - Once loaded, the inactive terms appear.

### S1-36 · C723900 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723900
- Title (old = new): No new permission appears on Roles & Permissions

There is no new toggle for credit terms: Credit Terms is governed by the existing "Finance" sub-row under "Settings"; Move Customers by the existing "Customers" row's "Create & Edit" column and the "View and Manage AP/AR Data" cross toggle.     - The role editor opens.
    - There is no new toggle for credit terms.
    - Credit Terms is governed by the existing "Finance" sub-row under "Settings".

### S2-21 · C723921 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723921
- Title (old = new): Make Default moves the tag and changes no existing customer
- **Old preconditions**

  - Write down the COD row's Customers count before you start.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Have the shop's own active term A (made for this test), due 14 days after the invoice date.
  - Make sure customer A (made for this test) holds COD, with a credit limit of $0.00.
  - Write down the COD row's Customers count before you start.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  Click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. Click "Make Default for New Customers".
  3. Click "Make Default".
  4. Open {Customer-A}'s customer page in a new tab.  →  Open customer A's customer page in a new tab.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens.
  - Step 2: the confirmation opens.
  - Step 3: the confirmation closes, the "Default" tag moves from COD to {Term-A}, and a green toast shows "{Term-A} is now the default" with the line "New customers start on {Term-A}." The COD row's Customers count is unchanged.
  - Step 4: {Customer-A}'s "Credit term" still reads COD.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The confirmation opens.
  - Step 3:
    - The confirmation closes, the "Default" tag moves from COD to term A, and a green toast shows "term A is now the default" with the line "New customers start on term A." The COD row's Customers count is unchanged.
  - Step 4:
    - Customer A's "Credit term" still reads COD.

### S2-22 · C723922 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723922
- Title (old = new): Deactivate acts at once and moves the term to the Inactive tab
- **Old preconditions**

  - Net 45 is active (customers and vendors may hold it).
  - Before you start, the Active tab's count reads {Count-A} and the Inactive tab's {Count-B} (write them down). _For example: {Count-A} is 14 and {Count-B} is 2._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Make sure Net 45 is active (customers and vendors may hold it).
  - Before you start, write down the Active tab's count (number A) and the Inactive tab's count (number B).
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "⋯" at the right of the "Net 45" row.
  2. Click "Deactivate".
  3. Click the "Inactive" tab.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens.

  - Step 3: Net 45 is listed on the Inactive tab in its usual place.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - No confirmation opens.
    - The Net 45 row leaves the Active tab, the Active count reads number A − 1 and the Inactive count number B + 1 (for example 13 and 3), and a green toast shows "Net 45 deactivated".
  - Step 3:
    - Net 45 is listed on the Inactive tab in its usual place.

### S2-28 · C723928 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723928
- Title (old = new): A long term name shows in full in the toast
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop switches off an unused term of its own that has the longest name allowed.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - The shop's own term {Term-A} has a 31-character name, due 14 days after the invoice date, and is unused. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S2-28 Long Name 0123 (31 characters)._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Make sure the shop's own term A (made for this test) has a 31-character name, due 14 days after the invoice date, and is unused.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  Click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. Click "Deactivate".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens.
  - Step 2: the toast reads "{Term-A} deactivated" with every character of the name; a long line wraps and nothing is cut off or replaced by "...".
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The toast reads "term A deactivated" with every character of the name.
    - A long line wraps and nothing is cut off or replaced by "...".

### S2-29 · C723929 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723929
- Title (old = new): Renaming the unused default term keeps it the default
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop's default term of its own is unused, and you give it a new name.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - The shop has its own active term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, the shop's default for new customers and held by no customer, vendor or invoice. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S2-29 Fourteen._
  - A new name for it, {Term-B}, that no term has. _For example: {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S2-29 Two Weeks._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have the shop's own active term A (made for this test), due 14 days after the invoice date, the shop's default for new customers and held by no customer, vendor or invoice.
  - Choose a new name for it, term B (made for this test), that no term has.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  Click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. Click "Edit".
  3. Replace the Name with {Term-B}.  →  Replace the Name with the name of term B.
  4. Click "Save Changes".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens and "Edit" is available.
  - Step 2: the "Edit Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 3: the Name reads {Term-B}.
  - Step 4: the window closes, the toast "{Term-B} saved" shows, and the row now reads {Term-B} with the "Default" tag still on it.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens and "Edit" is available.
  - Step 2:
    - The "Edit Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 3:
    - The Name reads term B.
  - Step 4:
    - The window closes, the toast "term B saved" shows, and the row now reads term B with the "Default" tag still on it.

### S2-30 · C723930 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723930
- Title (old = new): Deactivating a term another user just made the default is refused
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Two Admins work on credit terms at once; one makes a term the default while the other tries to switch it off.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - You and a second user, {User-B}, are both signed in with the "Admin" role, in two separate browsers. _For example: {User-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S2 Second Admin._
  - COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - The shop has its own active term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S2-30 Fourteen._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page is open on the Active tab in both browsers.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have a second user, user B, signed in with the "Admin" role in a second browser (you are signed in as an Admin in the first).
  - Make sure COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Have the shop's own active term A (made for this test), due 14 days after the invoice date.
  - Start with the Credit Terms page open on the Active tab in both browsers.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. In {User-B}'s browser, click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  In user B's browser, click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. In {User-B}'s browser, click "Make Default for New Customers".  →  In user B's browser, click "Make Default for New Customers".
  3. In {User-B}'s browser, click "Make Default".  →  In user B's browser, click "Make Default".
  4. In your browser, without reloading, click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  In your browser, without reloading, click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  5. Click "Deactivate".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: {User-B}'s menu opens.
  - Step 2: {User-B}'s confirmation opens.
  - Step 3: {User-B} sees the toast "{Term-A} is now the default".
  - Step 4: your menu still shows "Deactivate" available (your page has not reloaded).
  - Step 5: the app's error alert shows "This is your default for new customers. Pick another default first." and {Term-A} stays on the Active tab.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - User B's menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - User B's confirmation opens.
  - Step 3:
    - User B sees the toast "term A is now the default".
  - Step 4:
    - Your menu still shows "Deactivate" available (your page has not reloaded).
  - Step 5:
    - The app's error alert shows "This is your default for new customers. Pick another default first." and term A stays on the Active tab.

### S2-31 · C723931 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723931
- Title (old = new): Deleting a term another user just put a customer on is refused
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Two Admins work at once; one deletes an unused term while the other puts a customer on it.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - You and a second user, {User-B}, are both signed in with the "Admin" role, in two separate browsers. _For example: {User-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S2 Second Admin._
  - COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - The shop has its own active term {Term-A}, due 14 days after the invoice date, held by no customer, vendor or invoice. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S2-31 Fourteen._
  - Customer {Customer-A} holds COD. _For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S2-31 Customer._
  - Starting screen: your browser shows the Credit Terms page; {User-B}'s browser shows Edit Customer for {Customer-A}.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have a second user, user B, signed in with the "Admin" role in a second browser (you are signed in as an Admin in the first).
  - Make sure COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Have the shop's own active term A (made for this test), due 14 days after the invoice date, held by no customer, vendor or invoice.
  - Start with your browser showing the Credit Terms page and user B's browser showing Edit Customer for a customer who holds COD.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. In your browser, click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  In your browser, click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. Click "Delete Credit Term".
  3. In {User-B}'s browser, set Credit Terms to {Term-A}.  →  In user B's browser, set Credit Terms to term A.
  4. In {User-B}'s browser, click Save.  →  In user B's browser, click Save.
  5. In your browser, click the red "Delete Credit Term" button.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens.
  - Step 2: the delete confirmation opens.
  - Step 3: {Term-A} is selected in {User-B}'s Edit Customer.
  - Step 4: the customer is saved on {Term-A}.
  - Step 5: the app's error alert shows "Used by 1 customer. Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." The confirmation stays open and {Term-A} is not deleted.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The delete confirmation opens.
  - Step 3:
    - Term A is selected in user B's Edit Customer.
  - Step 4:
    - The customer is saved on term A.
  - Step 5:
    - The app's error alert shows "Used by 1 customer. Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." The confirmation stays open and term A is not deleted.

### S3-01 · C723937 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723937
- Title (old = new): New Credit Term opens with Name focused and Net X days chosen
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has every standard term switched on, and you start a new credit term.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - All twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure all twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click "New Credit Term".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: a window titled "New Credit Term" opens. The cursor is in "Name" (required), with the hint "Up to 31 characters. Prints on invoices and goes to QuickBooks." under it. Below are exactly two options, one chosen at a time: "Due Net X days" with a Days box followed by "days after the invoice date", and "Due day of the following month" with a Day box followed by "of the following month". "Due Net X days" is chosen, both boxes are empty, and the Day box is disabled. The buttons are "Cancel" and "Create Credit Term", and "Create Credit Term" is disabled. There is no option for an early-payment discount.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - A window titled "New Credit Term" opens.
    - The cursor is in "Name" (required), with the hint "Up to 31 characters. Prints on invoices and goes to QuickBooks." under it.
    - Below are exactly two options, one chosen at a time: "Due Net X days" with a Days box followed by "days after the invoice date", and "Due day of the following month" with a Day box followed by "of the following month".
    - "Due Net X days" is chosen, both boxes are empty, and the Day box is disabled.
    - The buttons are "Cancel" and "Create Credit Term", and "Create Credit Term" is disabled.
    - There is no option for an early-payment discount.

### S3-03 · C723939 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723939
- Title (old = new): The blue line explains a Net term with today's date as the example
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: You start a new term due a number of days after the invoice date and read the example the window gives.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - Today's date on the calendar of {Location-A} is {Date-A}. _For example: {Date-A} is Oct 21._
  - {Date-B} is {Date-A} plus 14 days. _For example: {Date-B} is Nov 4 (Oct 21 + 14 days)._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Note today's date on the calendar of the test location (date A).
  - Work out date B: date A plus 14 days.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click "New Credit Term".
  2. Type "Net 14 Days" in Name.
  3. Type 14 in the Days box.
  4. Replace 14 with 0.
  5. Replace 0 with 15.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 2: the Name reads Net 14 Days.
  - Step 3: a blue information line shows below the options: "Net 14 Days means payment is due 14 days after the invoice date. An invoice dated {Date-A} is due {Date-B}." The dates read as a short month and day (for example "Oct 21" and "Nov 4").
  - Step 4: the blue line disappears while the number is invalid.
  - Step 5: the blue line comes back, now ending "is due" {Date-A} plus 15 days (for example Nov 5).
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The Name reads Net 14 Days.
  - Step 3:
    - A blue information line shows below the options: "Net 14 Days means payment is due 14 days after the invoice date. An invoice dated date A is due date B." The dates read as a short month and day (for example "Oct 21" and "Nov 4").
  - Step 4:
    - The blue line disappears while the number is invalid.
  - Step 5:
    - The blue line comes back, now ending "is due" date A plus 15 days (for example Nov 5).

### S3-06 · C723942 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723942
- Title (old = new): The blue line's example date follows the location you work in
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: You work at two locations whose calendars are on different dates, and start a new term at each.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - You are enrolled at {Location-B}, in a time zone where today's date is already {Date-B} while it is still {Date-A} at {Location-A}. Run the case at a time of day when the two dates differ. _For example: {Location-A} is in Edmonton, {Location-B} is in Auckland, {Date-A} is Oct 21 and {Date-B} is Oct 22._
  - Today's date on the calendar of {Location-A} is {Date-A}. _For example: {Date-A} is Oct 21._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure you are also enrolled at location B, in a time zone where today's date is already one day later than where you work now. Run the case at a time of day when the two dates differ.
  - Note today's date on the calendar of the test location (date A).
  - Note today's date on the calendar of location B (date B).
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click "New Credit Term".
  2. Type 14 in the Days box.
  3. Click "Cancel".
  4. Switch location to {Location-B} with the location switcher.  →  Switch location to location B with the location switcher.
  5. Click "New Credit Term".
  6. Type 14 in the Days box.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 2: the blue line reads "… An invoice dated {Date-A} is due …" ({Date-A} plus 14 days).
  - Step 3: the window closes.
  - Step 4: the app now works in {Location-B}.
  - Step 5: the "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 6: the blue line now reads "… An invoice dated {Date-B} is due …" ({Date-B} plus 14 days).
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The blue line reads "… An invoice dated date A is due …" (date A plus 14 days).
  - Step 3:
    - The window closes.
  - Step 4:
    - The app now works in location B.
  - Step 5:
    - The "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 6:
    - The blue line now reads "… An invoice dated date B is due …" (date B plus 14 days).

### S3-08 · C723944 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723944
- Title (old = new): Creating a Net term shows a toast and puts it in every picker
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop creates a new term due 14 days after the invoice date and then puts a customer and a vendor on it.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - All twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - A new name {Term-A} that no term has, and a customer {Customer-A} and a vendor {Vendor-A}. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S3-08 Fourteen, {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S3-08 Customer and {Vendor-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S3-08 Vendor._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure all twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
  - Choose a new name for term A (made for this test) that no term has.
  - Create customer A and vendor A for this test.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click "New Credit Term".
  2. Type {Term-A} in Name.  →  Type the name of term A in Name.
  3. Type 14 in the Days box.
  4. Click "Create Credit Term".
  5. Open Edit Customer for {Customer-A} in a new tab.  →  Open Edit Customer for customer A in a new tab.
  6. Open the Credit Terms dropdown.
  7. Open Edit Vendor for {Vendor-A} in a new tab.  →  Open Edit Vendor for vendor A in a new tab.
  8. Open the Credit Terms dropdown.
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 2: the Name reads {Term-A}.
  - Step 3: the blue information line shows below the options.
  - Step 4: the window closes and a green toast shows "{Term-A} created". {Term-A} appears on the Active tab between Net 7 and Net 15, its Due reads "14 days after the invoice date", and its Customers and Vendors read 0 and 0.
  - Step 5: Edit Customer opens.
  - Step 6: {Term-A} is offered, between Net 7 and Net 15, without reloading anything else.
  - Step 7: Edit Vendor opens.
  - Step 8: {Term-A} is offered there too, between Net 7 and Net 15.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The Name reads term A.
  - Step 3:
    - The blue information line shows below the options.
  - Step 4:
    - The window closes and a green toast shows "term A created".
    - Term A appears on the Active tab between Net 7 and Net 15, its Due reads "14 days after the invoice date", and its Customers and Vendors read 0 and 0.
  - Step 5:
    - Edit Customer opens.
  - Step 6:
    - Term A is offered, between Net 7 and Net 15, without reloading anything else.
  - Step 7:
    - Edit Vendor opens.
  - Step 8:
    - Term A is offered there too, between Net 7 and Net 15.

### S3-14 · C723950 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723950

  - The shop's own term {Term-B}, due on day 5 of the following month, is deactivated. _For example: {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S3-14 Fifth._

  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**

  - Have the shop's own active term A, due 14 days after the invoice date.
  - Make sure the shop's own term B, due on day 5 of the following month, is deactivated.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click "New Credit Term".
  2. Type {Term-A} in Name, in capital letters and with two spaces between its words.  →  Type the name of term A in Name, in capital letters and with two spaces between its words.

  4. Replace the Name with {Term-B}.  →  Replace the Name with the name of term B.
  5. Click the Days box.

  - Step 2: the Name shows what you typed.

  - Step 4: the Name reads {Term-B}.
  - Step 5: the message "A term called {Term-B} already exists." shows, although {Term-B} is inactive.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The "New Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The Name shows what you typed.
  - Step 3:
    - The message "A term called term A already exists." shows (with the existing term's own spelling), and "Create Credit Term" is disabled.
  - Step 4:
    - The Name reads term B.
  - Step 5:
    - The message "A term called term B already exists." shows, although term B is inactive.

### S3-19 · C723955 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723955

- **Old preconditions**

  - The shop has its own active term {Term-A}, due 10 days after the invoice date, held by no customer, vendor or invoice. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S3-19 Net 10._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  - Have the shop's own active term A (made for this test), due 10 days after the invoice date, held by no customer, vendor or invoice.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  3. Click "Save Changes" without changing anything.

    - The buttons are "Cancel" and "Save Changes".
  - Step 3:

  - A new name {Term-A} that no term has. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S3-25 Fourteen._

  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have a second user, user B, signed in with the "Admin" role in a second browser (you are signed in as an Admin in the first).
  - Choose a new name for term A (made for this test) that no term has.
  - Start with the Credit Terms page open in both browsers.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. In your browser, click "New Credit Term".

  3. In your browser, type 14 in the Days box.
  4. In {User-B}'s browser, click "New Credit Term".  →  In user B's browser, click "New Credit Term".
  5. In {User-B}'s browser, type {Term-A} in Name.  →  In user B's browser, type the name of term A in Name.
  6. In {User-B}'s browser, type 20 in the Days box.  →  In user B's browser, type 20 in the Days box.
  7. In {User-B}'s browser, click "Create Credit Term".  →  In user B's browser, click "Create Credit Term".

  - Step 3: the blue information line shows below the options.

  - Step 5: the Name reads {Term-A}.

  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have a second user, user B, signed in with the "Admin" role in a second browser (you are signed in as an Admin in the first).
  - Have the shop's own active term A (made for this test), due 14 days after the invoice date, held by no customer, vendor or invoice.
  - Start with your browser showing the Credit Terms page and user B's browser showing Edit Customer for a customer who holds COD.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. In your browser, click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  In your browser, click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. Click "Edit".
  3. Replace 14 with 21 in the Days box.
  4. In {User-B}'s browser, set Credit Terms to {Term-A}.  →  In user B's browser, set Credit Terms to term A.
  5. In {User-B}'s browser, click Save.  →  In user B's browser, click Save.
  6. In your browser, click "Save Changes".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens.
  - Step 2: the "Edit Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 3: the blue line shows 21 days.
  - Step 4: the dropdown shows {Term-A}.
  - Step 5: the customer is saved on {Term-A}.
  - Step 6: the app's error alert shows "Used by 1 customer. Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." The window stays open with 21 still typed, and the row still reads 14 days.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The "Edit Credit Term" window opens.
  - Step 3:
    - The blue line shows 21 days.
  - Step 4:
    - The dropdown shows term A.
  - Step 5:
    - The customer is saved on term A.
  - Step 6:
    - The app's error alert shows "Used by 1 customer. Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." The window stays open with 21 still typed, and the row still reads 14 days.

### S4-01 · C723965 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723965
- Title (old = new): Move Customers lists the term's customers A to Z, all ticked
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop's own term is held by three customers with different credit limits, and you open Move Customers for it.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - Net 30 is the shop's default for new customers.
  - The shop's own term {Term-A}, due 15 days after the invoice date, is held by exactly three customers, {Customer-A}, {Customer-B} and {Customer-C} (a new term holds only this case's customers), whose names are in that A to Z order, with credit limits of $500.00, $0.00 and $1,000.00. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 N15, {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 Alpha, {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 Bravo and {Customer-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 Charlie._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure Net 30 is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Make sure the shop's own term A, due 15 days after the invoice date, is held by exactly three customers, customer A, customer B and customer C (a new term holds only this case's customers), whose names are in that A to Z order, with credit limits of $500.00, $0.00 and $1,000.00.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  Click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. Click "Move Customers".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens.
  - Step 2: a window titled "Move Customers from {Term-A}" opens. It shows, top to bottom: a "Search customers" box; "Select All" and "Select None" with "3 selected" on the right; a list of {Customer-A}, {Customer-B} and {Customer-C} in that order, each with a ticked checkbox; a "Move To" dropdown reading Net 30, with the hint "Prefilled with your default for new customers." below it; and the buttons "Cancel" and "Move 3 Customers".
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - A window titled "Move Customers from term A" opens.
    - It shows, top to bottom: a "Search customers" box.
    - "Select All" and "Select None" with "3 selected" on the right.
    - A list of customer A, customer B and customer C in that order, each with a ticked checkbox.
    - A "Move To" dropdown reading Net 30, with the hint "Prefilled with your default for new customers." below it.
    - The buttons "Cancel" and "Move 3 Customers".

### S4-02 · C723966 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723966
- Title (old = new): Moving from COD lists blank-term customers but never Counter Sale
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop has customers on COD, one with no credit term at all, and the Counter Sale customer.

  - COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Customer {Customer-A} holds COD, customer {Customer-B} has a blank credit term, and the organization's Counter Sale customer, {Customer-C}, is on COD. _For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-02 Alpha, {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-02 Blank (imported with an empty term) and {Customer-C} is the built-in Counter Sale customer._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Make sure customer A holds COD, customer B has a blank credit term, and the organization's Counter Sale customer, customer C, is on COD.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "⋯" at the right of the "COD" row.
  2. Click "Move Customers".
  3. Type the start their names share in "Search customers".

  - Step 1: the menu opens.
  - Step 2: a window titled "Move Customers from COD" opens.
  - Step 3: the list narrows to the case's customers. The list shows {Customer-A} and {Customer-B} (the blank-term customer is listed under COD), never {Customer-C} (search for its name too: it is not there). "Move To" starts empty because COD is the default, and no hint shows.
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - A window titled "Move Customers from COD" opens.
  - Step 3:
    - The list narrows to the case's customers.
    - The list shows customer A and customer B (the blank-term customer is listed under COD), never customer C (search for its name too: it is not there).
    - "Move To" starts empty because COD is the default, and no hint shows.

### S4-04 · C723968 · https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/723968
- Title (old = new): Searching narrows the list, Enter moves nobody, no match says so
- **Old preconditions**
  - Scenario: Your shop's own term is held by three customers, and you search for one of them in Move Customers.
  - You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. _For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919._
  - Net 30 is the shop's default for new customers.
  - The shop's own term {Term-A}, due 15 days after the invoice date, is held by exactly three customers, {Customer-A}, {Customer-B} and {Customer-C} (a new term holds only this case's customers), whose names are in that A to Z order, with credit limits of $500.00, $0.00 and $1,000.00. _For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-04 N15, {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-04 Alpha, {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-04 Bravo and {Customer-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-04 Charlie._
  - Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **New preconditions**
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure Net 30 is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Make sure the shop's own term A, due 15 days after the invoice date, is held by exactly three customers made for this test (a new term holds only this case's customers); one of them is customer B, the last word of whose name is in neither of the other two names.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
- **Old steps** / **new steps**
  1. Click the "⋯" at the right of the {Term-A} row.  →  Click the "⋯" at the right of the term A row.
  2. Click "Move Customers".
  3. Type the last word of {Customer-B}'s name in lower-case letters in "Search customers".  →  Type the last word of customer B's name in lower-case letters in "Search customers".
  4. Press Enter.
  5. Replace the search text with "qqqq".
- **Old results**
  - Step 1: the menu opens.
  - Step 2: a window titled "Move Customers from {Term-A}" opens.
  - Step 3: only {Customer-B} is listed (the match ignores capitals).
  - Step 4: nothing is moved: the window stays open and no toast shows.
  - Step 5: the list area shows "No customers match your search."
- **New Expected Result**
  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - A window titled "Move Customers from term A" opens.
  - Step 3:
    - Only customer B is listed (the match ignores capitals).
  - Step 4:
    - Nothing is moved: the window stays open and no toast shows.
  - Step 5:
    - The list area shows "No customers match your search."
